for the stable ordinal
\mathbb{S}
.
Then (
38
) runs
\Theta\subset M_{\rho_{\mathbb{S}}}
for every
\mathbb{S}\in dom({\tt Q}_{\Pi})
.
This means
\Theta\subset M_{\partial{\tt Q}}:=\bigcap_{\kappa\in\partial{\tt Q}}M_{\kappa}
, where

\partial{\tt Q}=\{\max({\tt Q}(\mathbb{S})):\mathbb{S}\in dom({\tt Q}),{\tt Q}(\mathbb{S})\neq\emptyset\}.

Ordinals occurring in derivations are restricted to the set
M_{\partial{\tt Q}}
.

In section
6
for
\Pi^{1}_{1}
-reflection, an ordinal
\gamma_{0}
is a threshold, which means that
every ordinal occurring in derivations is in
{\cal H}_{\gamma_{0}}(0)
and
the subscript
\gamma\leq\gamma_{0}
in
{\cal H}_{\gamma}
, while
each
\rho\in{\tt Q}
exceeds
\gamma_{0}
in such a way that

{\tt p}_{0}(\rho)\geq\gamma_{0}
.
This ensures us that
{\cal H}_{\gamma}(M_{\rho})\subset M_{\rho}
.
In the end, inferences
({\rm rfl}(\rho,d,f))
are removed in Lemma
6.48

by moving outside
{\cal H}_{\gamma_{0}}(0)
.
Specifically
{\tt Q}\subset{\cal H}_{\gamma_{0}+\mathbb{S}}(0)
.

Now we have several (successor) stable ordinals
\mathbb{S},\mathbb{T},\ldots\in dom({\tt Q})
.
Inferences
({\rm stbl}(\mathbb{S}))
and their children
({\rm rfl}_{\mathbb{S}}(\rho,d,f))
are eliminated
first for bigger
\mathbb{S}>\mathbb{T}
, and then smaller ones
({\rm stbl}(\mathbb{T}))
.
Therefore we need assignment
dom({\tt Q})\ni\mathbb{S}\mapsto\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}
for
thresholds so that

\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}<\gamma_{\mathbb{T}}^{{\tt Q}}
if
\mathbb{S}>\mathbb{T}
.
This is done by gapping, i.e., a gap
\mathbb{I}\cdot 2^{a}
between
\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}
and

\gamma_{\mathbb{T}}^{{\tt Q}}

in advance, when

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta;{\tt Q}_{\Pi})\vdash^{*a}_{c}\Gamma;\Pi^{[\cdot]}

is embedded to

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta_{\Pi},{\tt Q})\vdash^{a}_{c,c,\gamma_{0}}\widehat{\Gamma},\widehat{\Pi}
, cf. Capping
7.29
.

Definition 7.19

A triple
({\tt Q},\gamma^{{\tt Q}},e^{{\tt Q}})

is said to be a
finite family for ordinals
\gamma_{0}
and
b_{1}

if
{\tt Q}
is a finite family in the sense of Definition
7.13
and the following conditions are met:

1.

\gamma^{{\tt Q}}
is a map
dom({\tt Q})\ni\mathbb{S}\mapsto\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}
such that

\gamma_{0}+\mathbb{I}^{2}>\gamma^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{S}}\geq\gamma_{0}
,

\gamma^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{S}}\geq\gamma^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{T}}+\mathbb{I}
for
\{\mathbb{S}<\mathbb{T}\}\subset dom({\tt Q})

and

\mathbb{S}\in{\cal H}_{\gamma^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{S}}+\mathbb{I}}
for
\mathbb{S}\in dom({\tt Q})
.

{\tt Q}
is said to have
gaps

\eta
if

\gamma^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{S}}\geq\gamma^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{T}}+\mathbb{I}\cdot\eta
holds
for
\{\mathbb{S}<\mathbb{T}\}\subset dom({\tt Q})
, and

\gamma^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{S}}\geq\gamma_{0}+\mathbb{I}\cdot\eta
for
\mathbb{S}\in dom({\tt Q})
.

2.

For each
\rho\in{\tt Q}(\mathbb{S})
,

m(\rho):\mathbb{I}\to\varphi_{\mathbb{I}}(0)
is special,
s(\rho)\leq b_{1}
,

\rho\in{\cal H}_{\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}+\mathbb{I}}(0)
,
and
\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}\leq{\tt p}_{0}(\rho)
.

3.

e^{{\tt Q}}
assigns an ordinal
e_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}\in{\cal H}_{\gamma^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{S}}+\mathbb{I}}\cap(\mathbb{S}+1)
to each
\mathbb{S}\in dom({\tt Q})
such that

\max(\{0\}\cup\{\rho\in{\tt Q}(\mathbb{S}):s(\rho)>\mathbb{S}\})<e^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{S}}

(62)

Let
e^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{S}}=\mathbb{S}
when
\mathbb{S}\not\in dom({\tt Q})
.

Definition 7.20

For a finite family
{\tt Q}
,
and for
A\simeq\bigvee(A_{\iota})_{\iota\in J}

[{\tt Q}]_{A^{(\rho)}}J=[{\tt Q}]_{\lnot A^{(\rho)}}J=[{\tt Q}]_{A}J\cap[\partial{\tt Q}]J\cap[\rho]J

where
[{\tt u}]J=J
and

[\partial{\tt Q}]J=\bigcap_{\kappa\in\partial{\tt Q}}[\kappa]J.

Definition 7.21

1.

For a finite family
{\tt Q}
, let

\partial{\tt Q}=\{\max({\tt Q}(\mathbb{S})):\mathbb{S}\in dom({\tt Q}),{\tt Q}(\mathbb{S})\neq\emptyset\}
and

M_{\partial{\tt Q}}=\bigcap_{\kappa\in\partial{\tt Q}}M_{\kappa}
.

2.

[{\tt Q}]_{A^{(\rho)}}J=[{\tt Q}]_{\lnot A^{(\rho)}}J=[{\tt Q}]_{A}J\cap[\partial{\tt Q}]J\cap[\rho]J

where

[{\tt u}]J=J
and

[\partial{\tt Q}]J=\bigcap_{\kappa\in\partial{\tt Q}}[\kappa]J
.

Definition 7.22

H_{\rho}^{{\tt Q}}(f,b_{1},\gamma,\Theta)

denotes the
resolvent class
for
{\tt Q}
,
\rho
,
special functions
f
,
ordinals
b_{1},\gamma
, and
finite sets
\Theta
of ordinals defined as follows:

\sigma\in H_{\rho}^{{\tt Q}}(f,\gamma,\Theta)
iff

\sigma\in{\cal H}_{\gamma+\mathbb{I}}(0)\cap\rho\cap M_{\partial{\tt Q}}
,

SC_{\mathbb{I}}(m(\sigma))\subset{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta]
,

\Theta\subset M_{\sigma}
,

\gamma\leq{\tt p}_{0}(\sigma)\leq{\tt p}_{0}(\rho)
and

m(\sigma)
is special such that

s(f)\leq s(m(\sigma))\leq b_{1}
,

f^{\prime}\leq(m(\sigma))^{\prime}
,
where
\sigma,\rho\prec\mathbb{S}
and

f\leq g\Leftrightarrow\forall i(f(i)\leq g(i))
.

We define another derivability relation
(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{a}_{c,\xi,\gamma_{0},b_{1}}\Gamma
,
where

c
is a bound of ranks of cut formulas,
and
\xi
a bound of ordinals
\mathbb{S}
in the inference rules
({\rm rfl}_{\mathbb{S}}(\rho,d,f,b_{1}))
.

Definition 7.23

Let

\Theta^{(\rho)}=\Theta\cap M_{\rho}
and

\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}=\Theta\cap M_{\partial{\tt Q}}
.
Let

a,b,c,\xi<\mathbb{I}
, a finite set
\Theta\subset\mathbb{I}
,
and

{\tt Q}
be a finite family for

\gamma_{0},b_{1}
such that

dom({\tt Q})\subset(\xi+1)
.

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{a}_{c,\xi,\gamma_{0},b_{1}}\Gamma
holds
for a sequent

\Gamma=\bigcup\{\Gamma_{\rho}^{(\rho)}:\rho\in\{{\tt u}\}\cup\bigcup{\tt Q}\}

if
\gamma\leq\gamma_{0}

\forall\rho\in\{{\tt u}\}\cup\bigcup{\tt Q}\left({\sf k}(\Gamma_{\rho})\subset\mathcal{H}_{\gamma}[\Theta^{(\rho)}]\cap\mathcal{H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}]\right)

(63)

\forall\mathbb{S}\in dom({\tt Q})\left(\{\gamma,a,c,\xi,\gamma_{0},b_{1}\}\subset\mathcal{H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{{\tt Q}(\mathbb{S})}]\cap\mathcal{H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}]\right)

(64)

\forall\{\mathbb{U}\leq\mathbb{S}\}\subset dom({\tt Q})\left(\{\mathbb{S},\gamma^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{S}}\}\subset\mathcal{H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{{\tt Q}(\mathbb{U})}]\cap\mathcal{H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}]\right)

(65)

\forall\rho\in\{{\tt u}\}\cup\bigcup{\tt Q}\forall\mathbb{S}\in dom({\tt Q})\left({\sf k}^{\mathbb{S}}(\Gamma_{\rho})\subset\mathcal{H}_{\gamma}\left[\Theta_{{\tt Q}(\mathbb{S})}\right]\cap\mathcal{H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}]\right)

(66)

\forall(\mathbb{S},\rho)\in{\tt Q}\left(SC_{\mathbb{I}}(m(\rho))\subset\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}}\left[\Theta^{(\rho)}\cup\{\mathbb{S}\}\cup\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}\right]\right)

(67)

and one of the following cases holds:

(Taut)

\{\lnot A^{(\rho)},A^{(\rho)}\}\subset\Gamma
for
a
\rho\in\{{\tt u}\}\cup\bigcup{\tt Q}
and a formula
A
such that
\mbox{{\rm rk}}(A)<\mathbb{S}\leq\xi

for some successor stable ordinal
\mathbb{S}
.

If
\mbox{{\rm rk}}(A)<\mathbb{S}
, then
(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{0}_{0,\mathbb{S},\gamma_{0},b_{1}}\lnot A^{(\sigma)},A^{(\sigma)}
by (Taut) provided that (
64
) and (
66
) are met.

(\bigvee)

There exist

A\simeq\bigvee(A_{\iota})_{\iota\in J}
, a cap
\rho\in\{{\tt u}\}\cup\bigcup{\tt Q}
, an ordinal

a(\iota)<a
and an
\iota\in[\rho]J\cap[\partial{\tt Q}]J
such that

A^{(\rho)}\in\Gamma
and

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{a(\iota)}_{c,\xi,\gamma_{0},b_{1}}\Gamma,\left(A_{\iota}\right)^{(\rho)}
.

(\bigwedge)

There exist

A\simeq\bigwedge(A_{\iota})_{\iota\in J}
,
a cap
\rho\in\{{\tt u}\}\cup\bigcup{\tt Q}
, ordinals
a(\iota)<a

for each
\iota\in[{\tt Q}]_{A^{(\rho)}}J
such that

A^{(\rho)}\in\Gamma
and

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta\cup{\sf k}(\iota),{\tt Q})\vdash^{a(\iota)}_{c,\xi,\gamma_{0},b_{1}}\Gamma,\left(A_{\iota}\right)^{(\rho)}
.

(cut)

There exist a cap
\rho\in\{{\tt u}\}\cup\bigcup{\tt Q}
, ordinals
a_{0}<a
and a formula
C

such that

\mbox{{\rm rk}}(C)<c
,

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{a_{0}}_{c,\xi,\gamma_{0},b_{1}}\Gamma,\lnot C^{(\rho)}

and

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{a_{0}}_{c,\xi,\gamma_{0},b_{1}}C^{(\rho)},\Gamma
.

(\Sigma(\Omega)\mbox{{\rm-rfl}})

There exist ordinals

a_{\ell},a_{r}<a
and an uncapped formula
C\in\Sigma(\Omega)

such that

c\geq\Omega
,

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{a_{\ell}}_{c,\xi,\gamma_{0},b_{1}}\Gamma,C

and

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{a_{r}}_{c,\xi,\gamma_{0},b_{1}}\lnot\exists x<\pi\,C^{(x,\Omega)},\Gamma
.

({\rm rfl}_{\mathbb{S}}(\rho,d,f,b_{1}))

There exist a successor stable ordinal
\mathbb{S}\leq\xi

and an ordinal
\rho\prec\mathbb{S}
such that

\Theta_{{\tt Q}(\mathbb{S})}\cup\{\mathbb{S}\}\cup\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}\subset M_{\rho}

(68)

and

\rho\in{\tt Q}(\mathbb{S})
if
\mathbb{S}\in dom({\tt Q})
.
Let
{\tt R}={\tt Q}
if
\mathbb{S}\in dom({\tt Q})
.
Otherwise
{\tt R}={\tt Q}\cup\{(\mathbb{S},\rho)\}
, where

{\tt Q}\cup\{(\mathbb{S},\rho)\}
is a finite family for
\gamma_{0}

extending
{\tt Q}
such that

dom({\tt R})=dom({\tt Q})\cup\{\mathbb{S}\}
,

{\tt R}(\mathbb{S})={\tt Q}(\mathbb{S})\cup\{\rho\}
,

e^{{\tt R}}_{\mathbb{T}}=e^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{T}}
for
\mathbb{S}\neq\mathbb{T}\in dom({\tt Q})
,

\gamma^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{T}}\geq\gamma^{{\tt R}}_{\mathbb{S}}+\mathbb{I}
for every
\mathbb{S}>\mathbb{T}\in dom({\tt Q})

and
\gamma^{{\tt R}}_{\mathbb{S}}\geq\gamma_{0}+\mathbb{I}
.

Also there exist
an ordinal

d\in{\rm supp}(m(\rho))
,
a special function
f
,
an ordinal
a_{0}<a
,
and a
finite set
\Delta
of uncapped formulas enjoying the following conditions.

(r0)

\rho<e^{{\tt R}}_{\mathbb{S}}

if
s(\rho)=\max({\rm supp}(m(\rho)))>\mathbb{S}
.

(r1)

\Delta\subset\bigvee_{\mathbb{S}}\left(d\right):=\{\delta:\mbox{{\rm rk}}(\delta)<d,\delta\mbox{ is a }\bigvee\mbox{-formula}\}\cup\{\delta:\mbox{{\rm rk}}(\delta)<\mathbb{S}\}
.

(r2)

For
g=m(\rho)
,
s(f)\leq b_{1}
,

SC_{\mathbb{I}}(f)\cup SC_{\mathbb{I}}(g)\subset\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt R}}}[\Theta^{(\rho)}]
and

f_{d}=g_{d}\,\&\,f^{d}<_{\mathbb{I}}^{d}g^{\prime}(d)
.

(r3)

For each
\delta\in\Delta
,

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt R})\vdash^{a_{0}}_{c,\xi,\gamma_{0}}\Gamma,\lnot\delta^{(\rho)}
.

(r4)

Let
\gamma^{{\tt R}\cup\{(\mathbb{S},\sigma)\}}=\gamma^{{\tt R}}
,

e^{{\tt R}\cup\{(\mathbb{S},\sigma)\}}=e^{{\tt R}}
and

\sigma\in H^{{\tt R}}_{\rho}(f,b_{1},\gamma^{{\tt R}}_{\mathbb{S}},\Theta^{(\rho)}\cup\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}})
.
Then

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta\cup\{\sigma\},{\tt R}\cup\{(\mathbb{S},\sigma)\})\vdash^{a_{0}}_{c,\xi,\gamma_{0}}\Gamma,\Delta^{(\sigma)}
holds.

In particular

\sigma<e^{{\tt R}}_{\mathbb{S}}
if
s(\sigma)>\mathbb{S}

by (
62
).

Note that

\bigcup{\tt Q}\subset\mathcal{H}_{\gamma}[\Theta]

need not to hold.
Moreover
(\Theta\cup\{\sigma\})_{({\tt R}(\mathbb{S})\cup\{\sigma\})}=\Theta_{{\tt R}(\mathbb{S})}=\Theta_{{\tt Q}(\mathbb{S})}

and
\Theta_{\partial{\tt R}}=\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}

by

\Theta^{(\rho)}\subset M_{\sigma}
and (
68
).

In this subsection the ordinals
\gamma_{0}
and
b_{1}
will be fixed, and
we write
\vdash^{a}_{c,\xi}
for
\vdash^{a}_{c,\xi,\gamma_{0},b_{1}}
.

Lemma 7.24

(Tautology)

Let
\{\gamma,\gamma_{0},\mathbb{S}\}\cup{\sf k}^{\mathbb{T}}(A)\subset{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{{\tt Q}(\mathbb{T})}]\cap{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}]
for every

\mathbb{T}\in dom({\tt Q})\subset(\mathbb{S}+1)
,

\sigma\in\{{\tt u}\}\cup\bigcup{\tt Q}
and
{\sf k}(A)\subset M_{\sigma}
.
Then

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{2d}_{0,\mathbb{S}}\lnot A^{(\sigma)},A^{(\sigma)}
holds for
d=\max\{\mathbb{S},\mbox{{\rm rk}}(A)\}
.

Lemma 7.25

(Inversion)

Let
A\simeq\bigwedge(A_{\iota})_{\iota\in J}
and

(\mathcal{H},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{a}_{c,\xi}\Gamma
with
A^{(\rho)}\in\Gamma

and there is no
\mathbb{S}\in SSt
such that
\mbox{{\rm rk}}(A)<\mathbb{S}\leq\xi
.
Then for any
\iota\in[{\tt Q}]_{A^{(\rho)}}J
,

(\mathcal{H},\Theta\cup{\sf k}(\iota),{\tt Q})\vdash^{a}_{c,\xi}\Gamma,\left(A_{\iota}\right)^{(\rho)}
.

Proof
. We need to assume that there is no
\mathbb{S}\in SSt
such that
\mbox{{\rm rk}}(A)<\mathbb{S}\leq\xi

due to (Taut).

\Box

Lemma 7.26

(Reduction)

Let
C\simeq\bigvee(C_{\iota})_{\iota\in J}
and

\Omega\leq\mbox{{\rm rk}}(C)\leq c
.
Assume

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q}\vdash^{a}_{c,\xi}\Gamma,\lnot C^{(\tau)}

and

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{b}_{c,\xi}C^{(\tau)},\Gamma

with
SSt\cap(c,\xi]=\emptyset
.

Then

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{a+b}_{c,\xi}\Gamma
.

Lemma 7.27

(Cut-elimination)

If

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{a}_{c+c_{1},\xi}\Gamma

with
\Omega\leq c<\mathbb{I}
,
\forall\mathbb{S}\in dom({\tt Q})(c\in{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{{\tt Q}(\mathbb{S})}]\cap{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}])
and
SSt\cap(c,\xi]=\emptyset
,
then
(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{\varphi_{c_{1}}(a)}_{c,\xi}\Gamma
.

Lemma 7.28

(Collapsing)

Let

\Gamma\subset\Sigma(\Omega)

be a sets of uncapped formulas.
Suppose

\Theta\subset\mathcal{H}_{\gamma}(\psi_{\Omega}(\gamma))

and

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,\emptyset)\vdash^{a}_{\Omega,0}\Gamma
.
Let

\beta=\psi_{\Omega}(\hat{a})
with
\hat{a}=\gamma+\omega^{a}<\gamma_{0}
.
Then

(\mathcal{H}_{\hat{a}+1},\Theta,\emptyset)\vdash^{\beta}_{\beta,0}\Gamma^{(\beta,\Omega)}
holds.

7.5
Eliminations of stable ordinals

Lemma 7.29

(Capping)

Let

\Gamma\cup\Pi\subset\Delta_{0}(\mathbb{I})
be a set of uncapped formulas.
Suppose

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta;{\tt Q}_{\Pi})\vdash^{*a}_{c,\gamma_{0}}\Gamma;\Pi^{[\cdot]}
, where

a,c<\mathbb{I}
,
dom({\tt Q}_{\Pi})\subset c
,

\Gamma=\Gamma_{{\tt u}}\cup\bigcup_{\mathbb{S}\in dom({\tt Q}_{\Pi})}\Gamma_{\mathbb{S}}
,

\Pi^{[\cdot]}=\bigcup_{(\mathbb{S},\sigma)\in{\tt Q}_{\Pi}}\Pi_{\sigma}^{[\sigma/\mathbb{S}]}
.

For each
\mathbb{S}\in dom({\tt Q}_{\Pi})
,
let

\rho_{\mathbb{S}}=\psi_{\mathbb{S}}^{g_{\mathbb{S}}}(\delta_{\mathbb{S}})

be an ordinal with an ordinal
\delta_{\mathbb{S}}\in{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta]
and
a special finite function

g_{\mathbb{S}}=m(\rho_{\mathbb{S}}):\mathbb{I}\to\varphi_{\mathbb{I}}(0)
such that

{\rm supp}(g_{\mathbb{S}})=\{c\}
with
g_{\mathbb{S}}(c)=\alpha_{\mathbb{S}}+\mathbb{I}
,

\mathbb{I}(2a+1)\leq\alpha_{\mathbb{S}}+\mathbb{I}
,

SC_{\mathbb{I}}(g_{\mathbb{S}})=SC_{\mathbb{I}}(c,\alpha_{\mathbb{S}})\subset\mathcal{H}_{0}(SC_{\mathbb{I}}(\delta_{\mathbb{S}}))\cap\mathcal{H}_{\gamma}[\Theta]
,
cf. (
55
) and (
67
).
Also let
\widehat{\Pi}=\bigcup_{(\mathbb{S},\sigma)\in{\tt Q}_{\Pi}}\Pi_{\sigma}^{(\sigma)}
,

\widehat{\Gamma}=\Gamma_{{\tt u}}^{({\tt u})}\cup\bigcup_{\mathbb{S}\in dom({\tt Q}_{\Pi})}\Gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{(\rho_{\mathbb{S}})}
.

Let
{\tt Q}
be a finite family for
\gamma_{0}\geq\gamma
such that

{\tt Q}(\mathbb{S})={\tt Q}_{\Pi}(\mathbb{S})\cup\{\rho_{\mathbb{S}}\}
for
\mathbb{S}\in dom({\tt Q}_{\Pi})=dom({\tt Q})
,

\rho_{\mathbb{S}}\in\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}+\mathbb{I}}(0)
for
\mathbb{S}\in dom({\tt Q})
,
and

\alpha_{\mathbb{S}}+\mathbb{I}\leq\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}\leq\delta_{\mathbb{S}}<\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}+\mathbb{I}
.
Also
e^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{S}}=\rho_{\mathbb{S}}+1
.

Assume

\forall\mathbb{S}\in dom({\tt Q}_{\Pi})(\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}\in{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta])
,

{\tt Q}_{\Pi}(\mathbb{S})\subset\rho_{\mathbb{S}}
,

\Theta\cup\{\mathbb{S}\}\subset M_{\rho_{\mathbb{S}}}
,

{\tt p}_{0}(\sigma)\leq{\tt p}_{0}(\rho_{\mathbb{S}})=\delta_{\mathbb{S}}

and
SC_{\mathbb{I}}(m(\sigma))\subset{\cal H}_{\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}}[\Theta\cup\{\mathbb{S}\}]

for each
(\mathbb{S},\sigma)\in{\tt Q}_{\Pi}
,

\forall\{\mathbb{U}<\mathbb{S}\}\subset dom({\tt Q}_{\Pi})(\rho_{\mathbb{S}}\in M_{\rho_{\mathbb{U}}})
,
and
{\tt Q}
has gaps
2^{a}
.

Then

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta_{\Pi},{\tt Q})\vdash^{a}_{c,c,\gamma_{0},c}\widehat{\Gamma},\widehat{\Pi}
holds for

\Theta_{\Pi}=\Theta\cup\bigcup{\tt Q}_{\Pi}
.

Remark 7.30

When
\alpha_{\mathbb{S}}=\mathbb{I}(2a)
and
\Theta=\emptyset
,

\delta_{\mathbb{S}}<\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}+\mathbb{I}
denotes the natural sum

\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}\#a\#c
.
Then

\Theta\cup\{\mathbb{S}\}\subset M_{\rho_{\mathbb{S}}}
and

\{a,c\}\subset\mathcal{H}_{0}(SC_{\mathbb{I}}(\delta_{\mathbb{S}}))
.
Hence
(
55
) is enjoyed for
\rho_{\mathbb{S}}
.
Namely

SC_{\mathbb{I}}(g_{\mathbb{S}})=\{c,\alpha_{\mathbb{S}}+\mathbb{I}\}\subset\mathcal{H}_{0}(SC_{\mathbb{I}}(\delta_{\mathbb{S}}))\subset\mathcal{H}_{\delta_{\mathbb{S}}}(SC_{\mathbb{I}}(\delta_{\mathbb{S}}))

holds.

Let
\mathbb{U}\in dom({\tt Q}_{\Pi})\cap\mathbb{S}
.
We have
\{\gamma_{0},\mathbb{S},a,c\}\subset{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{{\tt Q}_{\Pi}(\mathbb{U})}]

by (
58
).
We intend to be
\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}=\gamma_{0}+\mathbb{I}\cdot 2^{a}\cdot n

for
n=\#\{\mathbb{T}\in dom({\tt Q}):\mathbb{T}\geq\mathbb{S}\}
.
Then

\{\mathbb{S},a,c,\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}\}\subset{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{{\tt Q}_{\Pi}(\mathbb{U})}]\cap{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}]

for (
64
) and (
65
).

On the other hand we have
{\tt Q}_{\Pi}(\mathbb{S})\subset\rho_{\mathbb{S}}
, and

\rho_{\mathbb{S}}=\max({\tt Q}(\mathbb{S}))
, i.e.,
\partial{\tt Q}=\{\rho_{\mathbb{S}}:\mathbb{S}\in dom({\tt Q}_{\Pi})\}
.
Also
\{\mathbb{S},\delta_{\mathbb{S}}\}\cup SC_{\mathbb{I}}(g_{\mathbb{S}})\subset{\cal H}_{0}(\{\mathbb{S},a,c,\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}\}\cup\Theta)\subset M_{\rho_{\mathbb{U}}}={\cal H}_{\delta_{\mathbb{U}}}(\rho_{\mathbb{U}})
for
\mathbb{U}\leq\mathbb{S}
.
Therefore
\rho_{\mathbb{S}}\in M_{\rho_{\mathbb{U}}}
for
\mathbb{U}<\mathbb{S}
by

\delta_{\mathbb{S}},\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}+\mathbb{I}\leq\gamma_{\mathbb{U}}^{{\tt Q}}
.
Moreover
\rho_{\mathbb{S}}\in M_{\rho_{\mathbb{U}}}
for
\mathbb{U}>\mathbb{S}
since

\rho_{\mathbb{S}}<\mathbb{S}<\rho_{\mathbb{U}}
.

Proof
of Lemma
7.29
.
This is seen
by induction on
a
as in Capping
6.44
.
Let us
write
\vdash^{a}_{c}
for
\vdash^{a}_{c,c,\gamma_{0},c}
in the proof.
By assumptions we have

{\tt Q}_{\Pi}(\mathbb{S})\subset\rho_{\mathbb{S}}
and

\Theta\subset M_{\rho_{\mathbb{S}}}
.
Hence
\Theta=\Theta^{(\rho_{\mathbb{S}})}=\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}
and

\Theta_{{\tt Q}_{\Pi}(\mathbb{S})}=\Theta_{{\tt Q}(\mathbb{S})}
.
On the other hand we have

{\sf k}(\Gamma)\subset{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta]
and for

\sigma\in\bigcup{\tt Q}_{\Pi}
,
{\sf k}(\Pi_{\sigma})\subset{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta^{(\sigma)}]

by (
57
).
Therefore (
63
) and (
66
) are enjoyed.
We have
\{\gamma,a,c,\gamma_{0},\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}},\mathbb{S}\}\subset{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{{\tt Q}_{\Pi}(\mathbb{U})}]\cap{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}]

for
every
\{\mathbb{U}\leq\mathbb{S}\}\subset dom({\tt Q})=dom({\tt Q}_{\Pi})
by the assumption,
(
58
) and (
59
).
Hence (
64
) and (
65
) are enjoyed.
Moreover for (
67
) we have

SC_{\mathbb{I}}(m(\rho_{\mathbb{S}}))\subset{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta]
and
\gamma\leq\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}
.

Case 1
.
First consider the case when the last inference is a
({\rm stbl}(\mathbb{S}))
:
We have a successor stable ordinal
\mathbb{S}
,
an ordinal
a_{0}<a
,
a
\bigwedge
-formula

B(0)\in\Delta_{0}(\mathbb{S})
, and
a term
u\in Tm(\mathbb{I})
with
\mathbb{S}\leq\mbox{{\rm rk}}(B(u))<c
.

For every ordinal
\sigma
such that

\Theta\cup\{\mathbb{S}\}\subset M_{\sigma}
and
{\tt p}_{0}(\sigma)\geq\gamma_{0}

({\cal H}_{\gamma},\Theta;{\tt Q}_{\Pi})\vdash^{*a}_{c}\Gamma;\Pi^{[\cdot]}\lx@proof@logical@and(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q}_{\Pi})\vdash^{*a_{0}}_{c}\Gamma,B(u);\Pi^{[\cdot]}(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta\cup\{\mathbb{S},\sigma\};{\tt Q}_{\Pi}\cup\{(\mathbb{S},\sigma)\})\vdash^{*a_{0}}_{c}\Gamma;\lnot B(u)^{[\sigma/\mathbb{S}]},\Pi^{[\cdot]}

Let
h
be a special finite function such that
{\rm supp}(h)=\{c\}
and

h(c)=\mathbb{I}(2a_{0}+1)
.
Then
h_{c}=(g_{\mathbb{S}})_{c}=\emptyset
and
h^{c}<_{\mathbb{I}}^{c}(g_{\mathbb{S}})^{\prime}(c)

by
h(c)=\mathbb{I}(2a_{0}+1)<\mathbb{I}(2a)\leq\alpha_{0}=(g_{\mathbb{S}})^{\prime}(c)
.
Let
{\tt R}={\tt Q}\cup\{(\mathbb{S},\rho_{\mathbb{S}})\}

and
\sigma\in H^{{\tt R}}_{\rho_{\mathbb{S}}}(h,c,\gamma^{{\tt R}}_{\mathbb{S}},\Theta^{(\rho_{\mathbb{S}})}\cup\{\mathbb{S}\}\cup\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}})
,
where

\Theta^{(\rho_{\mathbb{S}})}\cup\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}=\Theta
.

For example let

\sigma=\psi_{\rho_{\mathbb{S}}}^{h}(\delta_{\mathbb{S}}+\eta)
with
\eta=\max(\{1\}\cup E_{\mathbb{S}}(\Theta))
.
We obtain
\Theta\cup\{\mathbb{S}\}\subset{\cal H}_{\delta_{\mathbb{S}}}(\sigma)=M_{\sigma}
by
\Theta\cup\{\mathbb{S}\}\subset M_{\rho}
, and

\{\delta_{\mathbb{S}},a_{0},c\}\subset\mathcal{H}_{\gamma}[\Theta]
.
Let
\rho_{\mathbb{U}}\in\partial{{\tt R}}
. We claim that
\sigma\in M_{\rho_{\mathbb{U}}}
.
If
\mathbb{U}\geq\mathbb{S}
, then
\sigma<\rho_{\mathbb{U}}
.
Let
\mathbb{U}<\mathbb{S}
. Then we have
\rho_{\mathbb{S}}\in M_{\rho_{\mathbb{U}}}
by the assumption,
and
\sigma\in M_{\rho_{\mathbb{U}}}
follows from

\{c,a_{0},\delta_{\mathbb{S}}\}\cup\Theta\subset{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta]\subset{\cal H}_{\delta_{\mathbb{U}}}(\rho_{\mathbb{U}})

and
\delta_{\mathbb{S}}+\eta<\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}+\mathbb{I}\leq\gamma_{\mathbb{U}}^{{\tt Q}}\leq\delta_{\mathbb{U}}
.
Therefore
\sigma\in H^{{\tt R}}_{\rho_{\mathbb{S}}}(h,c,\gamma^{{\tt R}}_{\mathbb{S}},\Theta^{(\rho_{\mathbb{S}})}\cup\{\mathbb{S}\}\cup\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}})
.

Since
{\tt Q}
is assumed to have gaps
2^{a}
,
we may assume that

{\tt R}\cup\{(\mathbb{S},\sigma)\}
as well as
{\tt R}

has gaps
2^{a_{0}}
.

IH yields

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta_{\Pi},{\tt R})\vdash^{a_{0}}_{c}\widehat{\Gamma},B(u)^{(\rho_{\mathbb{S}})},\widehat{\Pi}
, and for
u^{[\sigma/\mathbb{S}]}\in Tm(\mathbb{S})
and

B(u^{[\sigma,\mathbb{S}]})\equiv B(u)^{[\sigma,\mathbb{S}]}
,

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta_{\Pi}\cup\{\mathbb{S},\sigma\},{\tt R}\cup\{(\mathbb{S},\sigma)\})\vdash^{a_{0}}_{c}\widehat{\Gamma},\lnot B(u)^{(\sigma)},\widehat{\Pi}
follows, where
\rho_{\mathbb{S}}>\sigma\in M_{\rho_{\mathbb{S}}}
and we have by (
59
),

{\sf k}(B(u))\subset{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{{\tt Q}_{\Pi}(\mathbb{T})}]\cap{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}]

if
\mbox{{\rm rk}}(B(u))\geq\mathbb{T}
.
Hence
{\sf k}(B(u))\subset{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{{\tt R}(\mathbb{T})}]\cap{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}]
by
\Theta_{{\tt R}(\mathbb{T})}=\Theta_{{\tt Q}_{\Pi}(\mathbb{T})}

for (
59
).
Moreover
we have

\mathbb{S}\in{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{{\tt Q}(\mathbb{T})}]
for every
\mathbb{T}<c
,

\Theta_{{\tt Q}_{\Pi}(\mathbb{S})}\cup\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}\subset M_{\rho_{\mathbb{S}}}
for (
68
),

\rho_{\mathbb{S}}<e^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{S}}

for (r0),

\mbox{{\rm rk}}(B(u))<c
and
s(\rho_{\mathbb{S}})\leq c
for (r1).

We obtain by an inference
({\rm rfl}_{\mathbb{S}}(\rho_{\mathbb{S}},c,h,c))

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta_{\Pi},{\tt Q})\vdash^{a}_{c}\widehat{\Gamma},\widehat{\Pi}\lx@proof@logical@and(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta_{\Pi},{\tt R})\vdash^{a_{0}}_{c}\widehat{\Gamma},B(u)^{(\rho_{\mathbb{S}})},\widehat{\Pi}(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta_{\Pi}\cup\{\mathbb{S},\sigma\},{\tt R}\cup\{(\mathbb{S},\sigma)\})\vdash^{a_{0}}_{c}\widehat{\Gamma},\lnot B(u)^{(\sigma)},\widehat{\Pi}

in the right upper sequents

\sigma
ranges over the resolvent class

\sigma\in H^{{\tt R}}_{\rho_{\mathbb{S}}}(h,c,\gamma^{{\tt R}}_{\mathbb{S}},\Theta^{(\rho_{\mathbb{S}})}\cup\{\mathbb{S}\}\cup\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}})
.

Case 2
.
When the last inference is a

(cut)
:
There exist
a_{0}<a
and
C

such that
\mbox{{\rm rk}}(C)<c
,

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta;{\tt Q}_{\Pi})\vdash^{*a_{0}}_{c}\Gamma,\lnot C;\Pi^{[\cdot]}

and

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta;{\tt Q}_{\Pi})\vdash^{*a_{0}}_{c}\Gamma,C;\Pi^{[\cdot]}
.
IH followed by a
(cut)
with an uncapped cut formula
C^{({\tt u})}
yields the lemma.

Case 3
.
Third the last inference introduces
a
\bigvee
-formula
A
in
\Gamma
.
Let
A\simeq\bigvee\left(A_{\iota}\right)_{\iota\in J}
.
Then
A^{(\rho_{\mathbb{S}})}\in\Gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{(\rho_{\mathbb{S}})}
.
There are an
\iota\in J
, an ordinal

a(\iota)<a

such that

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta;{\tt Q}_{\Pi})\vdash^{*a(\iota)}_{c}\Gamma,A_{\iota};\Pi^{[\cdot]}
.
We can assume
{\sf k}(\iota)\subset{\sf k}(A_{\iota})
, and claim that

\iota\in[\partial{\tt Q}]J
with
\rho_{\mathbb{S}}\in\partial{\tt Q}
.
We obtain

{\sf k}(\iota)\subset{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}]\subset M_{\partial{\tt Q}}

by (
57
) for
\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}=\Theta

and

\gamma\leq\gamma_{0}\leq\gamma^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{S}}\leq\delta_{\mathbb{S}}\leq{\tt p}_{0}(\rho_{\mathbb{S}})
.

IH yields
(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{a(\iota)}_{c}\widehat{\Gamma},(A_{\iota})^{(\rho_{\mathbb{S}})},\widehat{\Pi}
.

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{a}_{c}\widehat{\Gamma},\widehat{\Pi}

follows from a
(\bigvee)
.

Other cases are seen from IH as in Capping
6.44
.

Lemma 7.31

(Recapping)

Let
\mathbb{S}
be a successor stable ordinal,

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{a}_{c_{1},\mathbb{S},\gamma_{0},b_{2}}\Pi,\widehat{\Gamma}
with a finite family
{\tt Q}
for
\gamma_{0},b_{2}
,
\Gamma\cup\Pi\subset\Delta_{0}(\mathbb{I})
,
and
\widehat{\Gamma}=\bigcup\{\Gamma_{\rho}^{(\rho)}:\rho\in{\tt Q}^{t}(\mathbb{S})\}
, where
each
\theta\in\widehat{\Gamma}
is either a
\bigvee
-formula or
\mbox{{\rm rk}}(\theta)<\mathbb{S}
,

{\tt Q}^{t}\subset{\tt Q}

such that
{\tt Q}^{t}(\mathbb{S})\subset{\tt Q}(\mathbb{S})
with
dom({\tt Q}^{t})\subset\{\mathbb{S}\}

and
\forall\rho\in{\tt Q}^{t}(\mathbb{S})(s(\rho)>\mathbb{S})
, and

{\tt Q}^{f}
is a family such that
{\tt Q}^{f}(\mathbb{S})={\tt Q}(\mathbb{S})\setminus{\tt Q}^{t}(\mathbb{S})
and

{\tt Q}^{f}(\mathbb{T})={\tt Q}(\mathbb{T})
for
\mathbb{T}\neq\mathbb{S}
.

\Pi
is a set of formulas such that
\tau\in\{{\tt u}\}\cup\bigcup{\tt Q}^{f}
for every
A^{(\tau)}\in\Pi
.

Let
\max\{s(\rho):\rho\in{\tt Q}^{t}(\mathbb{S})\}\leq b_{1}
and
\omega(b,a)=\omega^{\omega^{b}}a
.
For each
\rho\in{\tt Q}^{t}(\mathbb{S})
, let
\mathbb{S}\leq b^{(\rho)}\in{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta^{(\rho)}]\cap{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}]

with
\mbox{{\rm rk}}(\Gamma_{\rho})<b^{(\rho)}<s(\rho)
,
and

\kappa(\rho)
be ordinals such that

\kappa(\rho)\in H^{{\tt Q}}_{\rho}(h^{b^{(\rho)}}(m(\rho);\omega(b_{1},a)),b_{2},\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}},\Theta^{(\rho)}\cup\{\mathbb{S}\}\cup\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}})
.
Assume

\forall\mathbb{T}\leq\mathbb{S}(b_{1}\in{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{{\tt Q}(\mathbb{T})}]\cap{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}])
.

Then

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q}^{\kappa})\vdash^{\omega(b_{1},a)}_{c_{b_{1}},\mathbb{S},\gamma_{0},b_{2}}\Pi,\widehat{\Gamma}_{\kappa}

holds,
where

\widehat{\Gamma}_{\kappa}=\bigcup\{\Gamma_{\rho}^{(\kappa(\rho))}:\rho\in{\tt Q}^{t}(\mathbb{S})\}
,

c_{b_{1}}=\max\{c_{1},b_{1}\}
,

{\tt Q}^{\kappa}={\tt Q}^{f}\cup\{(\mathbb{S},\kappa(\rho)):\rho\in{\tt Q}^{t}(\mathbb{S})\}
,

\gamma_{\mathbb{T}}^{{\tt Q}^{\kappa}}=\gamma_{\mathbb{T}}^{{\tt Q}}
,

e^{{\tt Q}^{\kappa}}_{\mathbb{T}}=e^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{T}}
for
\mathbb{T}\neq\mathbb{S}
and

e^{{\tt Q}^{\kappa}}_{\mathbb{S}}=\max(\{\tau\in{\tt Q}^{f}(\mathbb{S}):s(\tau)>\mathbb{S}\}\cup\{\kappa(\rho):\rho\in{\tt Q}^{t}(\mathbb{S})\})+1
.

e^{{\tt Q}^{\kappa}}_{\mathbb{S}}<e^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{S}}
holds
when

{\tt Q}^{t}=\{(\mathbb{S},\rho)\in{\tt Q}:s(\rho)>\mathbb{S}\}\neq\emptyset
.

Proof
. This is shown by main induction on
b_{1}

with subsidiary induction on
a
as in Recapping
6.47
.

Lemma 7.32

(Elimination of one stable ordinal)

Let
\mathbb{S}=\mathbb{T}^{\dagger}
be a successor stable ordinal and

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{a}_{\mathbb{S},\mathbb{S},\gamma_{0},b_{1}}\Pi,\widehat{\Gamma}
with a finite family
{\tt Q}
for
\gamma_{0}
and

b_{1}\geq\mathbb{S}
,
\Pi\subset\Delta_{0}(\mathbb{I})
,

\Gamma\subset\Delta_{0}(\mathbb{S})
,

\widehat{\Gamma}=\bigcup\{\Gamma_{\rho}^{(\rho)}:\rho\in{\tt Q}(\mathbb{S})\}
,
and

{\tt Q}^{t}=\{(\mathbb{S},\tau)\in{\tt Q}:s(\tau)>\mathbb{S}\}
,

{\tt Q}^{f}={\tt Q}\setminus{\tt Q}^{t}
.

\Pi
is a set of formulas such that
for each
A^{(\tau)}\in\Pi
,

\tau\in\{{\tt u}\}\cup\bigcup_{\mathbb{U}<\mathbb{S}}{\tt Q}(\mathbb{U})
.

Let
\tilde{a}=\varphi_{b_{1}+e^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{S}}}(a)
,

{\tt Q}_{1}={\tt Q}\!\upharpoonright\!\mathbb{S}=\{(\mathbb{T},\rho)\in{\tt Q}:\mathbb{T}<\mathbb{S}\}

\gamma_{1}=\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}+\mathbb{I}<\gamma_{0}+\mathbb{I}^{2}

Then

{\tt Q}_{1}
is a finite family for
\gamma_{1},b_{1}
and

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{1}},\Theta,{\tt Q}_{1})\vdash^{\tilde{a}}_{\mathbb{T},\mathbb{T},\gamma_{1},b_{1}}\Pi,\Gamma^{({\tt u})}

holds for

\Gamma^{({\tt u})}=\bigcup\{\Gamma_{\rho}^{({\tt u})}:\rho\in{\tt Q}(\mathbb{S})\}

Proof
. This is seen
by main induction on
e^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{S}}
with subsidiary induction on
a
as in Lemma
6.48
.
When
\mathbb{S}\in dom({\tt Q})
, we have
{\tt Q}(\mathbb{S})\subset{\cal H}_{\gamma_{1}}
and

e^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{S}}\in{\cal H}_{\gamma_{1}}
for
\gamma_{1}=\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}+\mathbb{I}

by Definition
7.19
.

{\tt Q}_{1}
is a finite family for
\gamma_{1},b_{1}
.
Then
\gamma_{1}\in{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{{\tt Q}_{1}(\mathbb{T})}]\cap{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}]
for every
\mathbb{T}\in dom({\tt Q}_{1})

by (
64
).

First assume
{\tt Q}^{t}(\mathbb{S})\neq\emptyset
.
For each
\rho\in{\tt Q}^{t}(\mathbb{S})
, let

\kappa(\rho)
be an ordinal such that

\kappa(\rho)\in H^{{\tt Q}}_{\rho}(h^{\mathbb{S}}(m(\rho);\omega(b_{1},a)),b_{1},\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}},\Theta^{(\rho)}\cup\{\mathbb{S}\}\cup\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}})

with
\omega(b,a)=\omega^{\omega^{b}}a
.
We obtain
({\cal H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q}^{\kappa})\vdash^{\omega(b_{1},a)}_{b_{1},\mathbb{S},\gamma_{0},b_{1}}\Pi,\widehat{\Gamma}_{\kappa}

by Recapping
7.31
.
Cut-elimination
7.27
with
SSt\cap(\mathbb{S},\mathbb{S}]=\emptyset

yields for
a_{1}=\varphi_{b_{1}}(\omega(b_{1},a))
,

({\cal H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q}^{\kappa})\vdash^{a_{1}}_{\mathbb{S},\mathbb{S},\gamma_{0},b_{1}}\Pi,\widehat{\Gamma}_{\kappa}
,
where

e^{{\tt Q}^{\kappa}}_{\mathbb{S}}=\max\{\kappa(\rho):\rho\in{\tt Q}^{t}(\mathbb{S})\}+1<e^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{S}}
.
MIH yields

({\cal H}_{\gamma_{1}},\Theta,{\tt Q}_{1})\vdash^{\tilde{a}_{1}}_{\mathbb{T},\mathbb{T},\gamma_{1},b_{1}}\Pi,\Gamma^{({\tt u})}
, where

\tilde{a}_{1}=\varphi_{b_{1}+e^{{\tt Q}^{\kappa}}_{\mathbb{S}}}(a_{1})<\varphi_{b_{1}+e^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{S}}}(a)
and
\gamma_{1}=\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}+\mathbb{I}
.

In what follows assume
{\tt Q}^{t}(\mathbb{S})=\emptyset
.

Case 1
.
First let
\{\lnot A^{(\sigma)},A^{(\sigma)}\}\subset\Pi\cup\widehat{\Gamma}
with
\sigma\in\{{\tt u}\}\cup\bigcup{\tt Q}
and

d=\mbox{{\rm rk}}(A)<\mathbb{S}
by (Taut).
If

, then

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{1}},\Theta,{\tt Q}_{1})\vdash^{\tilde{a}}_{\mathbb{T},\mathbb{T},\gamma_{1},b_{1}}\Pi,\Gamma^{({\tt u})}

by (Taut).

Let
\mathbb{T}\leq d<\mathbb{S}
.

({\cal H}_{\gamma_{1}},\Theta,{\tt Q}_{1})\vdash^{2d}_{0,\mathbb{T},\gamma_{1},b_{1}}\Pi,\Gamma^{({\tt u})}

7.24
and

({\cal H}_{\gamma_{1}},\Theta,{\tt Q}_{1})\vdash^{\tilde{a}}_{0,\mathbb{T},\gamma_{1},b_{1}}\Pi,\Gamma^{({\tt u})}

by
\tilde{a}>\mathbb{S}>d
.

Case 2
.
Second consider the case when the last inference is a
({\rm rfl}_{\mathbb{U}}(\rho,d,f,b_{1}))
.
If
\mathbb{U}\leq\mathbb{T}
, then SIH followed by a
({\rm rfl}_{\mathbb{U}}(\rho,d,f,b_{1}))
yields the lemma.
Let
\mathbb{U}=\mathbb{S}
.

Let

g=m(\rho)
and
s(\rho)\geq d\in{\rm supp}(g)
.
Let

and
\gamma_{1}=\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt R}}+\mathbb{I}
.
We have a sequent

for each
\delta\in\Delta
.
On the other hand we have

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta\cup\{\sigma\},{\tt R}\cup\{(\mathbb{S},\sigma)\})\vdash^{a_{0}}_{\mathbb{S},\mathbb{S},\gamma_{0},b_{1}}\Pi,\widehat{\Gamma},\Delta^{(\sigma)}
, where

\sigma\in H^{{\tt Q}}_{\rho}(f,b_{1},\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt R}},\Theta^{(\rho)}\cup\{\mathbb{S}\}\cup\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}})
,

f
is a special finite function such that
s(f)\leq b_{1}
,

f_{d}=g_{d}
,
f^{d}<^{d}g^{\prime}(d)
and

SC_{\mathbb{I}}(f)\subset\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt R}}}[\Theta^{(\rho)}]
.

Case 2.1
.
s(\rho)\leq\mathbb{S}
: Then
\Delta\subset\Delta_{0}(\mathbb{S})
.
Let
\tilde{a}_{0}=\varphi_{b_{1}+e^{{\tt R}}_{\mathbb{S}}}(a_{0})
.
SIH yields
(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{1}},\Theta,{\tt Q}_{1})\vdash^{\tilde{a}_{0}}_{\mathbb{T},\mathbb{T},\gamma_{1},b_{1}}\Pi,\Gamma^{({\tt u})},\lnot\delta^{({\tt u})}

\delta\in\Delta
, and

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{1}},\Theta\cup\{\sigma\},{\tt Q}_{1})\vdash^{\tilde{a}_{0}}_{\mathbb{T},\mathbb{T},\gamma_{1},b_{1}}\Pi,\Gamma^{({\tt u})},\Delta^{({\tt u})}
for
\sigma\in{\cal H}_{\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt R}}+\mathbb{I}}={\cal H}_{\gamma_{1}}
.

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{1}},\Theta,{\tt Q}_{1})\vdash^{\tilde{a}_{0}+p}_{\mathbb{S},\mathbb{T},\gamma_{1},b_{1}}\Pi,\Gamma^{({\tt u})}

(cut)

,
where
\varphi_{\mathbb{S}}(\tilde{a}_{0}+p)<\tilde{a}=\varphi_{b_{1}+e^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{S}}}(a)
by
b_{1}+e_{\mathbb{S}}^{{\tt Q}}>\mathbb{S}
.

Case 2.2
.
s(\rho)>\mathbb{S}
: Then
\mathbb{S}\not\in dom({\tt Q})
and
\Gamma=\emptyset
.
We have

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt R})\vdash^{a}_{\mathbb{S},\mathbb{S},\gamma_{0},b_{1}}\Pi
.

{\tt R}^{t}=\{(\mathbb{S},\rho)\}
.

7.31

Case 3
.
The last inference is a
(\bigwedge)
:
We have
a(\iota)<a
,
A^{(\rho)}\in\widehat{\Gamma}
and for each

(\bigwedge)
yields

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{1}},\Theta,{\tt Q}_{1})\vdash^{\tilde{a}}_{\mathbb{T},\mathbb{T},\gamma_{1},b_{1}}\Pi,\Gamma^{({\tt u})}

Other cases are seen from SIH.

Definition 7.33

We define the
S-rank

{\rm srk}(A^{(\rho)})
of a capped formula
A^{(\rho)}
as follows.
Let
{\rm srk}(A^{({\tt u})})=0
, and

{\rm srk}(A^{(\rho)})=\mathbb{S}
for
\rho\prec\mathbb{S}\in SSt
.

{\rm srk}(\Gamma)=\max\{{\rm srk}(A^{(\rho)}):A^{(\rho)}\in\Gamma\}
.

Lemma 7.34

(Elimination of stable ordinals)

Suppose

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{a}_{\xi,\xi,\gamma_{0},b_{1}}\Gamma
and

{\rm srk}(\Gamma)\leq\mathbb{S}<\xi\leq b_{1}<\mathbb{I}
, where
\mathbb{S}
is either a stable ordinal or
\mathbb{S}=\Omega
such that

\forall\mathbb{U}\in dom({\tt Q}_{\mathbb{S}})(\mathbb{S}\in{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{{\tt Q}(\mathbb{U})}]\cap{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}])
for
{\tt Q}_{\mathbb{S}}={\tt Q}\!\upharpoonright\!\mathbb{S}
.

Then there exists an ordinal
\gamma_{0}\leq\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}<\gamma_{0}+\mathbb{I}^{2}
such that

{\tt Q}_{\mathbb{S}}
is a finite family for
\gamma_{\mathbb{S}},b_{1}
and

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}},\Theta,{\tt Q}_{\mathbb{S}})\vdash^{f(\xi,a)}_{\mathbb{S},\mathbb{S},\gamma_{\mathbb{S}},b_{1}}\Gamma
holds for
f(\xi,a)=\varphi_{b_{1}+\xi+1}(a)
.

Proof
. By main induction on
\xi
with subsidiary induction on
a
.
(
64
) in

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}},\Theta,{\tt Q}_{\mathbb{S}})\vdash^{f(\xi,a)}_{\mathbb{S},\mathbb{S},\gamma_{\mathbb{S}},b_{1}}\Gamma

follows from (
64
) and (
65
) in
(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{a}_{\xi,\xi,\gamma_{0},b_{1}}\Gamma
.

Case 1
.
Consider the case when the last inference is a
({\rm rfl}_{\mathbb{T}}(\rho,d,f,b_{1}))

for a
\mathbb{T}=\mathbb{U}^{\dagger}\leq\xi
.
If
\mathbb{T}\leq\mathbb{S}
, then SIH yields the lemma.
Let
\mathbb{S}<\mathbb{T}\in dom({\tt R})
for
{\tt R}={\tt Q}\cup\{(\mathbb{T},\rho)\}
.
We have
\forall\mathbb{U}\in dom({\tt Q}_{\mathbb{T}})(\mathbb{T}\in{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{{\tt Q}(\mathbb{U})}]\cap{\cal H}_{\gamma}[\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}}])
by (
64
).
Let
\Delta
be a finite set of sentences such that

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt R})\vdash^{a_{0}}_{\xi,\xi,\gamma_{0},b_{1}}\Gamma,\lnot\delta^{(\rho)}

for each
\delta\in\Delta
, and

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt R}\cup\{(\mathbb{T},\sigma)\})\vdash^{a_{0}}_{\xi,\xi,\gamma_{0},b_{1}}\Gamma,\Delta^{(\sigma)}

for each
\sigma\in H^{{\tt Q}}_{\rho}(f,b_{1}\gamma_{\mathbb{T}}^{{\tt R}},\Theta^{(\rho)}\cup\{\mathbb{T}\}\cup\Theta_{\partial{\tt Q}})
, and

a_{0}<a
.
We have
{\rm srk}(\delta^{(\rho)})={\rm srk}(\Delta^{(\sigma)})=\mathbb{T}
.
By SIH there exists a
\gamma_{\mathbb{T}}<\gamma_{0}+\mathbb{I}^{2}
such that for
a_{1}=f(\xi,a_{0})=\varphi_{b_{1}+\xi+1}(a_{0})
,

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{\mathbb{T}}},\Theta,{\tt Q}_{\mathbb{T}})\vdash^{a_{1}}_{\mathbb{T},\mathbb{T},\gamma_{\mathbb{T}},b_{1}}\Gamma,\lnot\delta^{(\rho)}

for each
\delta\in\Delta
, and

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{\mathbb{T}}},\Theta,{\tt Q}_{\mathbb{T}}\cup\{(\mathbb{T},\sigma)\})\vdash^{a_{1}}_{\mathbb{T},\mathbb{T},\gamma_{\mathbb{T}},b_{1}}\Gamma,\Delta^{(\sigma)}
.

({\rm rfl}_{\mathbb{T}}(\rho,d,f,b_{1}))
yields

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{\mathbb{T}}},\Theta,{\tt Q}_{\mathbb{T}})\vdash^{a_{2}}_{\mathbb{T},\mathbb{T},\gamma_{\mathbb{T}},b_{1}}\Gamma
for
a_{2}=a_{1}+1
.

On the other hand we have

{\rm srk}(\Gamma)\leq\mathbb{S}<\mathbb{T}=\mathbb{U}^{\dagger}\leq\xi
.
By Lemma
7.32
pick a
\gamma_{\mathbb{U}}<\gamma_{\mathbb{T}}+\mathbb{I}^{2}=\gamma_{0}+\mathbb{I}^{2}
such that

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{\mathbb{U}}},\Theta,{\tt Q}_{\mathbb{U}})\vdash^{a_{3}}_{\mathbb{U},\mathbb{U},\gamma_{\mathbb{U}},b_{1}}\Gamma
, where

a_{3}=\varphi_{b_{1}+e^{{\tt Q}_{1}}_{\mathbb{T}}}(a_{2})=\varphi_{b_{1}+e^{{\tt Q}_{1}}_{\mathbb{T}}}(f(\xi,a_{0})+1)<\varphi_{b_{1}+\xi+1}(a)=f(\xi,a)
by
e^{{\tt Q}_{1}}_{\mathbb{T}}\leq\mathbb{T}\leq\xi
.
If
\mathbb{S}=\mathbb{U}
, then we are done.
Let
\mathbb{S}<\mathbb{U}
with
\mathbb{U}<\xi
.
Then by MIH pick a
\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}
such that

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}},\Theta,{\tt Q}_{\mathbb{S}})\vdash^{a_{4}}_{\mathbb{S},\mathbb{S},\gamma_{\mathbb{S}},b_{1}}\Gamma
for

a_{4}=f(\mathbb{U},a_{3})=\varphi_{b_{1}+\mathbb{U}+1}(a_{3})<\varphi_{b_{1}+\xi+1}(a)=f(\xi,a)

by
\mathbb{U}<\xi
.

Case 2
. Next consider the case when the last inference is a
(cut)
of a cut formula

C^{(\sigma)}
wth
\mbox{{\rm rk}}(C)<\xi
and
\mathbb{T}={\rm srk}(C^{(\sigma)})\leq\xi
.
We have an ordinal
a_{0}<a
such that

and

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma},\Theta,{\tt Q})\vdash^{a_{0}}_{\xi,\xi,\gamma_{0},b_{1}}C^{(\sigma)},\Gamma
.

Let

.

\mathbb{U}<\xi
.
By SIH pick a

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{\mathbb{U}}},\Theta,{\tt Q}_{\mathbb{U}})\vdash^{a_{1}}_{\mathbb{U},\mathbb{U},\gamma_{\mathbb{U}},b_{1}}C^{(\sigma)},\Gamma
, where

.
A
(cut)
yields

.

with

yields

, where

by
\xi<b_{1}+\xi+1
.
If

, then we are done.

. By MIH with

we obtain

for a
\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}
,

by
\mathbb{U}<\xi
.

Second let
\mathbb{T}=\mathbb{U}=\xi=\mathbb{W}^{\dagger}>\mathbb{S}
. Then
C\in\Delta_{0}(\mathbb{T})
.
By Lemma
7.32
pick a
\gamma_{\mathbb{W}}
such that

, where

.
A
(cut)
yields

, and we obtain

by Cut-elimination

, where

and

\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}
such that

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{\mathbb{S}}},\Theta,{\tt Q}_{\mathbb{S}})\vdash^{a_{5}}_{\mathbb{S},\mathbb{S},\gamma_{\mathbb{S}},b_{1}}\Gamma

,
\mathbb{T}=\xi<b_{1}+\xi+1
,
e^{{\tt Q}}_{\mathbb{T}}\leq\mathbb{T}=\xi<\xi+1
and
a_{0}<a
.

Case 3
. There exists an
A
such that
\{\lnot A^{(\rho)},A^{(\rho)}\}\subset\Gamma

.

Other cases are seen from SIH.

Theorem 7.35

Suppose
{\sf KP}\omega+\Pi_{1}\mbox{{\rm-Collection}}+(V=L)\vdash\theta^{L_{\Omega}}

for a
\Sigma_{1}
-sentence
\theta
.
Then

L_{\psi_{\Omega}(\varepsilon_{\mathbb{I}+1})}\models\theta
holds.

Proof
. Let

S_{\mathbb{I}}\vdash\theta^{L_{\Omega}}
for a
\Sigma
-sentence
\theta
.
By Embedding
7.16
pick an
m>0
so that

(\mathcal{H}_{\mathbb{I}},\emptyset;\emptyset)\vdash_{\mathbb{I}+m}^{*\mathbb{I}\cdot 2+m}\theta^{L_{\Omega}}
.
Cut-elimination
7.17
yields

(\mathcal{H}_{\mathbb{I}},\emptyset;\emptyset)\vdash_{\mathbb{I}}^{*a}\theta^{L_{\Omega}}

for
a=\omega_{m}(\mathbb{I}\cdot 2+m)<\omega_{m+1}(\mathbb{I}+1)
.
Then Collapsing
7.18
yields

(\mathcal{H}_{\hat{a}+1},\emptyset;\emptyset)\vdash_{\beta}^{*\beta}\theta^{L_{\Omega}}

for
\beta=\psi_{\mathbb{I}}(\hat{a})\in LS
with

\hat{a}=\omega^{\mathbb{I}+a}=\omega_{m+1}(\mathbb{I}\cdot 2+m)>\beta
.
Capping
7.29
then yields

(\mathcal{H}_{\hat{a}+1},\emptyset,\emptyset)\vdash_{\beta,\beta,\gamma_{0},\beta}^{\beta}\theta^{L_{\Omega}}

where
\gamma_{0}=\hat{a}+1
and
\theta^{L_{\Omega}}\equiv(\theta^{L_{\Omega}})^{({\tt u})}
.

Let

\alpha=\varphi_{\beta\cdot 2+1}(\beta)
.
By Lemma
7.34
we obtain

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{\Omega}},\emptyset,\emptyset)\vdash_{\Omega,\Omega,\gamma_{\Omega},\beta}^{\alpha}\theta^{L_{\Omega}}
for a
\gamma_{\Omega}<\gamma_{0}+\mathbb{I}^{2}
.
This means

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{\Omega}},\emptyset,\emptyset)\vdash_{\Omega,0,\gamma_{\Omega},\beta}^{\alpha}\theta^{L_{\Omega}}
.

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{\Omega}+\alpha+1},\emptyset,\emptyset)\vdash_{\delta,0,\gamma_{\Omega},\beta}^{\delta}\theta^{L_{\delta}}
follows from Collapsing
7.28

for

\delta=\psi_{\Omega}(\gamma_{\Omega}+\alpha)
with
\omega^{\alpha}=\alpha
.
Cut-elimination
7.27
yields

(\mathcal{H}_{\gamma_{\Omega}+\alpha+1},\emptyset,\emptyset)\vdash_{0,0,\gamma_{\Omega},\beta}^{\varphi_{\delta}(\delta)}\theta^{L_{\delta}}
.
We see that
\theta^{L_{\delta}}
is true by induction up to
\varphi_{\delta}(\delta)
,
where
\delta<\psi_{\Omega}(\omega_{m+2}(\mathbb{I}+1))<\psi_{\Omega}(\varepsilon_{\mathbb{I}+1})
.

7.6
Well-foundedness proof in
\Sigma^{1}_{3}\mbox{{\rm-DC+BI}}

Theorem 7.36

[
A

∞

c
]

\Sigma^{1}_{3}\mbox{{\rm-DC+BI}}\vdash Wo[\alpha]

for
each

\alpha<\psi_{\Omega}(\varepsilon_{\mathbb{I}+1})
.

To prove Theorem
7.36
, let us introduce
1
-distinguished sets
D_{1}[X]
, which is obtained from
Definition
3.5
.
1
of distinguished sets
D[X]
,
first by replacing the next regular
\alpha^{+}
by the next stable
\alpha^{\dagger}
, and
second by changing the well-founded part
W({\cal C}^{\alpha}(X))
to the maximal
distinguished set
{\cal W}_{1}^{\alpha}(X)=\bigcup\{P:D_{0}^{\alpha}[P;X]\}
relative to
\alpha
and
X
,
where
P\cap\alpha=X\cap\alpha
if
D_{0}^{\alpha}[P;X]
and
\alpha
is stable.
We see that
{\cal W}=\bigcup\{X:D_{1}[X]\}
is the maximal
1
-distinguished and
\Sigma^{1}_{3}
-class.

In this subsection let us sketch a part of a well-foundeness proof in
\Sigma^{1}_{3}\mbox{{\rm-DC+BI}}

by pinpointing the lemma for which we need
\Sigma^{1}_{3}\mbox{{\rm-DC}}
.

An ordinal term
\sigma
in
OT(\mathbb{I})
is said to be
regular
if

\psi_{\sigma}^{f}(a)
is in
OT(\mathbb{I})
for some
f
and
a
.

Reg
denotes the set of regular terms.
In this section we need the next regular ordinal above an ordinal
\alpha

in defining distinguished sets.
Although it is customarily denoted by
\alpha^{+}
, it is hard to discriminate
\alpha^{+}
from
the next stable ordinal
\alpha^{\dagger}
.
Therefore let us write for
\alpha<\mathbb{I}
,

\alpha^{+^{1}}=\min\{\sigma\in SSt:\sigma>\alpha\}

for the next stable ordinal
\alpha^{\dagger}
, and

\alpha^{+^{0}}=\min\{\sigma\in Reg:\sigma>\alpha\}
for the next regular ordinal
\alpha^{+}
.
Let
\alpha^{+^{1}}:=\alpha^{+^{0}}:=\infty
if
\alpha\geq\mathbb{I}
.
Let

\alpha^{-^{1}}:=\max\{\sigma\in St_{\mathbb{I}}\cup\{0\}:\sigma\leq\alpha\}
when
\alpha<\mathbb{I}
, and

\alpha^{-^{1}}:=\mathbb{I}
if
\alpha\geq\mathbb{I}
.
Since
SSt\subset Reg
, we obtain
\alpha^{+^{0}}\leq\alpha^{+^{1}}
and

\beta^{+^{0}}<\sigma
if
\beta<\sigma\in St
since each
\sigma\in St
is a limit of regular ordinals.

Definition 7.37

\mathcal{C}^{\alpha}(X)
is the closure of

\{0,\Omega,\mathbb{I}\}\cup(X\cap\alpha)
under
+,\varphi
,

\{\sigma,\beta\}\cup SC_{\mathbb{I}}(f)\mapsto\psi_{\sigma}^{f}(\beta)
for
\sigma>\alpha
, and

\rho\mapsto\mathbb{I}[\rho],\rho^{\dagger}
for
\mathbb{I}[\rho],\rho^{\dagger}\geq\alpha
in
OT(\mathbb{I})
.

Definition 7.38

For
P,X\subset OT(\mathbb{I})
and

\gamma\in OT(\mathbb{I})\cap\mathbb{I}
, let

\displaystyle W_{0}^{\alpha}(P)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle W(\mathcal{C}^{\alpha}(P))

\displaystyle D_{0}^{\gamma}[P;X]

\displaystyle:\Leftrightarrow

\displaystyle P\cap\gamma^{-^{1}}=X\cap\gamma^{-^{1}}\,\&\,Wo[X\cap\gamma^{-^{1}}]\,\&\,

(69)

\displaystyle\forall\alpha\left(\gamma^{-^{1}}\leq\alpha\leq P\to W_{0}^{\alpha}(P)\cap\alpha^{+^{0}}=P\cap\alpha^{+^{0}}\right)

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{1}^{\gamma}(X)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\bigcup\{P\subset OT(\mathbb{I}):D_{0}^{\gamma}[P;X]\}

\displaystyle D_{1}[X]

\displaystyle:\Leftrightarrow

\displaystyle Wo[X]\,\&\,\forall\gamma\left(\gamma\leq X\to\mathcal{W}_{1}^{\gamma}(X)\cap\gamma^{+^{1}}=X\cap\gamma^{+^{1}}\right)

(70)

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{2}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\bigcup\{X\subset OT(\mathbb{I}):D_{1}[X]\}

A set
P
is said to be a
0
-
distinguished set
for
\gamma
and
X
if
D_{0}^{\gamma}[P;X]
,
and a set
X
is a
1
-

if
D_{1}[X]
.

Observe that in
\Sigma^{1}_{2}\mbox{{\rm-AC}}
,

W^{\alpha}_{0}(P)
is
\Pi^{1}_{1}
,

is
\Delta^{1}_{2}

Hence

is a
\Sigma^{1}_{3}
-class.

Let
\alpha\in P
for a
0
-distinguished set
P
for
\gamma<\mathbb{I}
and
X
.
If
\alpha<\gamma^{-^{1}}
, then
\alpha\in X
with
Wo[X]
.
Otherwise

W(\mathcal{C}^{\alpha}(P))\cap\alpha^{+^{0}}=W^{\alpha}_{0}(P)\cap\alpha^{+^{0}}=P\cap\alpha^{+^{0}}
with

.
Hence

is a well order.

Lemma 7.43

Assume
D_{1}[Y]
,
\mathbb{I}>\mathbb{S}\in Y\cap(St\cup\{0\})
and

\{0,\Omega\}\subset Y
.
Then
\mathbb{S}^{+^{1}}=\mathbb{S}^{\dagger}\in\mathcal{W}_{2}
.

Proof
. Since the condition (
72
) in Lemma
3.15

is fulfilled with
(\mathbb{S}^{+^{1}})^{-^{0}}=(\mathbb{S}^{+^{1}})^{-^{1}}=\mathbb{S}^{+^{1}}

\mathbb{S}^{-^{1}}=\mathbb{S}

it suffices to show that
\mathbb{S}^{+^{1}}\in\mathcal{G}^{Y}
.

\alpha=\mathbb{S}^{+^{1}}
.

.
Moreover

\gamma\in\mathcal{C}^{\alpha}(Y)\cap\alpha\Rightarrow\gamma\in Y
is seen by induction on

using the assumption

.
Therefore
\alpha\in\mathcal{G}^{Y}
.

Lemma 7.44

(\Sigma^{1}_{3}\mbox{{\rm-DC}})

If

\alpha\in\mathcal{G}^{\mathcal{W}_{2}}
,
then there exists a
1
-distinguished
set

Z
such that
\{0,\Omega\}\subset Z
,

\alpha\in\mathcal{G}^{Z}
and

\forall\mathbb{S}\in Z\cap(St\cup\{\Omega\})[\mathbb{S}^{\dagger}\in Z]
.

Proof
. Let
\alpha\in\mathcal{G}^{\mathcal{W}_{2}}
.
We have
\alpha\in\mathcal{C}^{\alpha}(\mathcal{W}_{2})
.
Pick a
1
-distinguished set
X_{0}
such that
\alpha\in\mathcal{C}^{\alpha}(X_{0})
.
We can assume
\{0,\Omega\}\subset X_{0}
.
On the other hand we have

\mathcal{C}^{\alpha}(\mathcal{W}_{2})\cap\alpha\subset\mathcal{W}_{2}

and

\forall\mathbb{S}\in\mathcal{W}_{2}\cap(St_{\mathbb{I}}\cup\{\Omega\})[\mathbb{S}^{\dagger}\in\mathcal{W}_{2}]

by Lemma
7.43
.
We obtain

\displaystyle\forall n\forall X\exists Y\{D_{1}[X]\to D_{1}[Y]

\displaystyle\land

\displaystyle\forall\beta\in OT(\mathbb{I})\left(\ell\beta\leq n\land\beta\in\mathcal{C}^{\alpha}(X)\cap\alpha\to\beta\in Y\right)

\displaystyle\land

\displaystyle\forall\mathbb{S}\in(St\cup\{\Omega\})\left(\ell\mathbb{S}\leq n\land\mathbb{S}\in X\to\mathbb{S}^{\dagger}\in Y\right)\}

Since
D_{1}[X]
is
\Delta^{1}_{3}
,

\Sigma^{1}_{3}\mbox{-DC}
yields a set
Z
such that
Z_{0}=X_{0}
and

\displaystyle\forall n\{D_{1}[Z_{n}]\to D_{1}[Z_{n+1}]

\displaystyle\land

\displaystyle\forall\beta\in OT(\mathbb{I})\left(\ell\beta\leq n\land\beta\in\mathcal{C}^{\alpha}(Z_{n})\cap\alpha\to\beta\in Z_{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle\land

\displaystyle\forall\mathbb{S}\in(St\cup\{\Omega\})\left(\ell\mathbb{S}\leq n\land\mathbb{S}\in Z_{n}\to\mathbb{S}^{\dagger}\in Z_{n+1}\right)\}

Let
Z=\bigcup_{n}Z_{n}
.
We see by induction on
n
that
D_{1}[Z_{n}]
for every
n
.
Lemma
7.40
.
3
yields
D_{1}[Z]
.
Let
\beta\in\mathcal{C}^{\alpha}(Z)\cap\alpha
.
Pick an
n
such that
\beta\in\mathcal{C}^{\alpha}(Z_{n})
and
\ell\beta\leq n
.
We obtain
\beta\in Z_{n+1}\subset Z
.
Therefore
\alpha\in\mathcal{G}^{Z}
.
Furthermore let
\mathbb{S}\in Z\cap(St\cup\{\Omega\})
.

n

and
\ell\mathbb{S}\leq n

We obtain

.

\Box

Remark 7.45

Lemma
7.44
is a
\Sigma^{1}_{4}
-statement, which is proved in

\Sigma^{1}_{3}\mbox{-DC}
.
Alternatively we could prove the lemma in
\Sigma^{1}_{3}\mbox{-AC}

if we assign fundamental sequences to limit ordinals as in
[
Jäger83
]
.
